  • Naming / reclassification papers
    • Reclassification of 'Thiobacillus prosperus' Huber and Stetter 1989 as Acidihalobacter prosperus gen. nov., sp. nov., a member of the family Ectothiorhodospiraceae. (2015, International journal of systematic and evolutionary microbiology, cited by 17)

(2019, International journal of systematic and evolutionary microbiology, cited by 15)
